# Graph Report - thingtime  (2026-10-20)

## Corpus Check
- 4271 files · ~10,421,827 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.

## Summary
- 56561 nodes · 151914 edges · 1665 communities (1387 shown, 278 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 97% EXTRACTED · 3% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 4241 edges (avg confidence: 0.72)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `76000584`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
